# Live uPD6383GF frame -- KN5000 IC311, program 0, UNIT-1 REVERB SEEDED, 2026-09-11.
# UPD6383_REVSEED=8 seeds the reverb's operand cells (0xD0/0x94/0x8A/0x85/0x8C/0x8F,
# >>7) at unit-1 entry so the reverb computes with a controlled stimulus -- the
# unit-1 analogue of BIQSEED, to decode the reverb one-pole WITHOUT the input route.
#
# REGENERATE (binary built with UPD6383_REVSEED, upd6383.cpp; needs MAME -log):
#   cd ~/compartilhado/kn7000_mame_build
#   NAV=0 AUDIO=key BOOTGATE=10 DWELL=28 DSPVAL=3 UPD6383_REVSEED=8 UPD6383_TRACE_FRAME=460000 \
#   timeout 110 ./kn7000 kn5000 -rompath ./roms -skip_gameinfo -log \
#     -autoboot_script ~/compartilhado/kn7000_mame/tools/rigs/kn5000_dsp_frame_trace.lua
#   sed 's/^\[:dsp1\] //' error.log > this-file
#
# RESULT: unit-1 goes 0 live operands/products -> 41 operands / 16 products, so the
# reverb DATAPATH is excitable by controlled stimulus (like the biquad).  The 16
# products are mostly the seed propagating via ACT 0x0E (P<-bus) at <<16 (=ACC_SHIFT,
# P=268435456=4096<<16).  ⚠ PARTIAL: the reverb 0.91 feedback MAC (n=142, SRC 0x1A)
# still gives P=0 -- it reads its operand via SRC 0x1A from a cell NOT in the seed
# set, so a full one-pole decode needs seeding that exact feedback cell.  This
# REOPENS Phase 5.1 (datapath decodable via seeding), correcting the earlier
# "downstream stages are uncapturable" over-claim.
#
